```
- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch
```

```
94	  /** AI_006 头部（L18645 通用常数 maxSpd=8 accel=0.07；穿墙直行；段链跟随） */
95	  private wormAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
96	    // 玩家死亡（:51532-51539）：TargetClosest 落空 → EncourageDespawn(300) 缓慢消散
97	    // （EoW flag=false 不加速下钻——靠 despawn 系统离屏倒数收尾）
98	    if (!player) {
99	      this.encourageDespawn(300);
100	      this.vx *= 0.97; this.vy *= 0.97;
101	      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
102	      this.visAngle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI * 0.5;
103	      return;
104	    }
105	    const maxSpd = 8, accel = 0.07;
106	    // 朝向：有玩家朝玩家，无玩家缓慢巡游
107	    let dx: number, dy: number;
108	    if (player) {
109	      // 375 TruffleWormDigger（松露虫钻地逃走形）：AI_006 反向——逃离玩家而非追击
110	      const s = this.vanillaId === 375 ? -1 : 1;
111	      dx = s * (player.cx - this.cx); dy = s * (player.cy - this.cy);
112	    }
113	    else { dx = Math.cos(this.aiT * 0.02) * 10; dy = Math.sin(this.aiT * 0.013) * 10; }
114	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
115	    this.vx += (dx / d) * accel;
116	    this.vy += (dy / d) * accel;
117	    const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
118	    if (spd > maxSpd) { this.vx = (this.vx / spd) * maxSpd; this.vy = (this.vy / spd) * maxSpd; }
119	    this.facing = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
120	    // 旋转（AI_006_Worms :52591 头/:51500 段）：贴图正面朝上 → rotation = atan2 + π/2。
121	    // 头朝目标（:52591 num49/50 = 朝向分量，等价速度角）；段用速度角（:51500）
122	    this.visAngle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI * 0.5;
123	    // 蠕虫穿墙：直接位移（原版 noTileCollide）
124	    this.x += this.vx;
125	    this.y += this.vy;
126	    // 段链跟随（原版 L52271-52308）：方向向量收缩维持 linkDist 间距——
127	    // shrink = (dist - linkDist)/dist；position += dxC*shrink（原版 num63/num64）
128	    for (let s = this.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
129	      const fx = s.wormFollow!;
130	      const dxC = fx.cx - s.cx;
131	      const dyC = fx.cy - s.cy;
132	      const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
133	      if (dist > 0.01) {
134	        const linkDist = s.w;               // 原版 num64 = width
135	        const shrink = (dist - linkDist) / dist;
136	        s.x += dxC * shrink;
137	        s.y += dyC * shrink;
138	        s.facing = dxC < 0 ? 1 : -1;         // 原版 spriteDirection（L52305）
139	      }
140	      // 段旋转 = 指向前一段的方向（= 本段行进切向，与原版段速度角等价）
141	      if (dist > 0.01) s.visAngle = Math.atan2(dyC, dxC) + Math.PI * 0.5;
142	    }
143	  }
144	
145	  /** 由头生成段链（原版各 worm 的 NewNPC 链，NPC.cs:18174+）：body×n + tail */
146	  static spawnWormChain(head: Enemy, segCount: number): Enemy[] {
147	    const segs: Enemy[] = [];
148	    const bodyId = head.vanillaId! + 1, tailId = head.vanillaId! + 2;
149	    let prev = head;
150	    for (let k = 0; k < segCount; k++) {
151	      const id = k === segCount - 1 ? tailId : bodyId;
152	      const s = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, head.cx, head.cy);
153	      if (!s) continue;
154	      s.wormFollow = prev;
155	      prev.wormNext = s;
156	      prev = s;
157	      segs.push(s);
158	    }
159	    return segs;
160	  }

163	  /** 用原版数据造怪：属性/碰撞/音效全部来自 SetDefaults 提取值 */
164	  static fromVanilla(id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy | null {
165	    const v = vanillaNpc(id);
166	    if (!v) return null;
167	    const e = new Enemy(`v_${id}`, x, y);
168	    e.vanillaId = id;
169	    e.vanilla = v;
170	    const hit = vanillaSoundName(v.HitSound) ?? 'NPC_Hit_1';
171	    const kill = vanillaSoundName(v.DeathSound) ?? 'NPC_Killed_1';
172	    const flying = v.noGravity || v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5 || v.aiStyle === 14;
173	    e.def = {
174	      ...e.def,
175	      // friendly（被缚 NPC 等城镇系）与 critter 一样零接触伤害——原版 friendly 旗
176	      name: v.name, hp: v.lifeMax, damage: (v.critter || v.friendly) ? 0 : v.damage, defense: v.defense,
177	      // 原版 knockBackResist 是"承受击退的比例"（0.5=吃一半）；本仓库语义是
178	      // "抗性"（hurt(): resist<0.9 才生效，kbx*(1-resist)）→ 换算 1-比例
179	      knockbackResist: Math.max(0, Math.min(0.89, 1 - (v.knockBackResist ?? 0.5))),
180	      width: Math.round(v.width * (v.scale ?? 1)), height: Math.round(v.height * (v.scale ?? 1)), flying,
181	      boss: VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id),
182	      nightOnly: v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5, underground: false,
183	      mapColor: '#9A8FA0', gore: ['#9A8FA0', '#5E5566', '#C4BACC'],
184	      hitSound: [hit], killedSound: [kill], drops: [], // 掉落走 NpcDrops 规则树（击杀时求值，不再预展开）
185	      // 小动物：无接触伤害、不夜行
186	      ...(v.critter ? { damage: 0, nightOnly: false } : {}),
187	    };
188	    e.hp = v.lifeMax;
189	    e.maxHp = v.lifeMax;
190	    // 碰撞盒乘 npc.scale(NPC.cs:17747-17748 width/height=(int)(×scale))——
191	    // 史莱姆王/毁灭者 1.25、血肉墙 1.2:此前未乘,贴图放大了盒子没放大,
192	    // 判定区比视觉小一圈(与渲染 scale 不一致的"盒贴图不匹配"来源)
193	    const vScale = v.scale ?? 1;
194	    e.w = Math.round(v.width * vScale);
195	    e.h = Math.round(v.height * vScale);
196	    e.spawnAlpha = v.alpha ?? 0; // 原版 SetDefaults alpha（静态不透明度，NPC.Opacity=1-alpha/255）
197	    // EoW 族 alpha=255 = 出生全透明渐显标记（其余 alpha 为静态不透明度,勿动）：
198	    // 钳到 254 并置 alphaFade,由 fixedUpdate 逐 tick 减回 0（原版 AI_006 渐显）
199	    if (e.spawnAlpha >= 255) { e.spawnAlpha = 254; e.alphaFade = true; }
200	    e.colorRGBA = v.color ? [v.color[0], v.color[1], v.color[2], v.color[3] ?? 255] : null; // 原版 color 字段
201	    e.x = x - e.w / 2;
202	    e.y = y - e.h / 2;
203	    return e;
204	  }

269	   *  无通用渐隐——仅特定家族（幽灵/怨灵等）在自己的 AI 内衰减 */
270	  spawnAlpha = 0;
271	  /** 出生渐显中（仅原版 alpha=255 的 EoW 族）;静态半透明(史莱姆 175 等)不参与 */
272	  alphaFade = false;
273	  /** 原版 ai[0] 跳跳计数器（史莱姆 hop 节奏）；ai[2] 卡墙禁追冷却 */
274	  ai0 = -1120;
275	  ai2 = 0;
276	  /** 原版 ai[1]（史莱姆体内物品 id，驱动 SLIME_ITEM_COLORS 渐变色；0=无） */
277	  ai1 = 0;
278	  /** 原版 ai[3]（地牢 AI 族用：诅咒头骨状态/火轮速度档/刺球相位） */
279	  ai3 = 0;
280	  /** 原版 directionY（火轮/刺球对角弹跳的纵向方向） */
281	  dirY = 1;
282	  /** AI 首帧初始化标记（原版 ai[0]==0 门在 ai0 初值 -1120 的史莱姆语义下不可用） */
283	  aiInit = false;
284	  /** 原版 timeLeft（CheckActive 屏内每 tick 重置；activeTime=750，非 7500） */
285	  despawnTimer = 750;
286	  /** Boss 离场逃走标记（EncourageDespawn/天亮撤离等非击杀消亡）：
287	   *  Game 记账分支据此区分「逃走」（不播击败公告、不写 downed 标志）与「被击败」 */
288	  bossFled = false;
289	  /** 原版 despawnEncouraged（EncourageDespawn 置位，屏内重置清除） */
290	  despawnEncouraged = false;
291	  inWater = false;       // 入水检测（溅落声用）
292	  /** Boss 专属初始化标记(部件生成等一次性动作;aiN 默认值有歧义故独立字段) */
293	  bInit = false;
294	  /** 部件 → 主体引用（骷髅王手 36 → 头：渲染臂骨 IK 与存活判定用） */
295	  master: Enemy | null = null;
296	
297	  constructor(public key: string, x: number, y: number) {
298	    super();
299	    this.def = ENEMY_DEFS[key] ?? PLACEHOLDER_DEF;
300	    this.hp = this.def.hp;
301	    this.maxHp = this.def.hp;
302	    this.w = this.def.width;
303	    this.h = this.def.height;
304	    this.x = x - this.w / 2;
305	    this.y = y - this.h / 2;
306	  }
307	
308	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
309	    this.prevX = this.x; this.prevY = this.y;
310	    // 联机傀儡（房主权威快照驱动）：无本地 AI——位置外推 + 动画计时，
311	    // 打击/拾取等交互经 entityNet 上报房主结算
312	    if (this.netPuppet) {
313	      this.aiT++;
314	      this.animT++;
315	      this.x += this.vx;
316	      this.y += this.vy;
317	      this.onGround = Math.abs(this.vy) < 0.6;
318	      if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.05) this.walkCycleT += Math.abs(this.vx);
319	      if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
320	      return;
321	    }
322	    this.aiT++;
323	    if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
324	    if (this.justHitT > 0) this.justHitT--;
325	    this.lightRGB = null; // 点光由 AI 每帧重写（不残留上一帧状态）
326	    if (this.hpBarT > 0) this.hpBarT--;
327	    if (this.squash !== 0) this.squash *= 0.85;
328	    this.animT++;
329	    this.walkCycleT += Math.abs(this.vx); // 原版 zombie/fighter 行走帧按 |vx| 累加（L77072/L70216）
330	
331	    // EoW 族出生渐显（每 tick alpha-1;原版 AI_006）——静态半透明不受影响
332	    if (this.alphaFade) {
333	      this.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, this.spawnAlpha - 1);
334	      if (this.spawnAlpha === 0) this.alphaFade = false;
335	    }
336	
337	    const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
338	    const hasPlayer = !!player && !player.dead;
339	
340	    // ---- 原版数据驱动路径：按 aiStyle 分发到已移植的 AI 家族（分发后落入共享尾段） ----
341	    // 蠕虫身体段（wormFollow 非空）无 AI：位置由头部 wormAI 沿链驱动，但仍走共享尾段（接触伤害等）
342	    // 小动物（critter）优先走被动游荡，绝不落入 default 的追击 fallback
343	    if (this.vanilla && !this.wormFollow && this.vanilla.critter) {
344	      this.critterWanderAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null);
345	    } else if (this.vanilla && !this.wormFollow) {
346	      const p = hasPlayer ? player : null;
347	      switch (this.vanilla.aiStyle) {
348	        case 92: this.dummyAI(game); break;                            // AI_092 训练假人（NPC 488）
349	        case 1: this.slimeAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_001 史莱姆
350	        case 2: this.floatEyeAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_002 飘浮眼（原版核）
351	        case 3: this.fighterAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_003 战士族（原版通用核）
352	        // EoC 传参例外:玩家死亡也要传原对象(cs:19931 dead 判定走逃离分支,
353	        // 冲刺计时合法冻结;传 null 会让 aiT 照走、状态机停摆 → 首冲时长翻倍)
354	        case 4: this.eocAI(game, (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player ?? null); break; // AI_004 克苏鲁之眼（NPC.cs:19909）
355	        case 5: this.swarmerAI(game, p); break;                         // AI_005 噬魂怪族（原版蜂群核）
356	        case 6: this.wormAI(game, p); break;                           // AI_006 蠕虫族（头）
357	        case 8: this.casterAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_008 法师族（传送+弹幕）
358	        case 10: this.cursedSkullAI(game, p); break;                     // AI_010 诅咒头骨（地牢飞行族）
359	        case 11:
360	          // 68 守卫走专用分支（bossAI.skeletronBossAI 注释"守卫分支已在 skeletronHeadAI"
361	          // 即此拆分：守卫恒速追击与骷髅王战斗状态机互不混用）
362	          if (this.vanillaId === 68) this.dungeonGuardianAI(game, p);
363	          else skeletronBossAI(this, game, p);
364	          break;                                                         // AI_011 骷髅王头战斗核/地牢守卫
365	        case 12: skeletronHandAI(this, game, p); break;                    // AI_012 骷髅王手(锚定头)
366	        case 20: this.blazingWheelAI(); break;                           // AI_020 火轮（地牢陷阱）
367	        case 21: this.spikeBallAI(game); break;                          // AI_021 刺球（地牢陷阱）
368	        case 14: this.batAI(game, p); break;                            // AI_014 蝙蝠/飞行族（1.4.5.6 原版核）
369	        case 16: this.swimAI(game, p); break;                           // AI_016 游泳族（鲨鱼/食人鱼）
370	        case 18: this.jellyfishAI(game, p); break;                      // AI_018 水母族（脉冲游动）
371	        case 22: this.ghostAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_022 幽灵族（穿墙缓速）
372	        case 13: this.plantAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_013 植物/攀爬者族（锚点伸展）
373	        case 15: kingSlimeAI(this, game, p); break;                       // AI_015 史莱姆王(大跳+小史莱姆)
374	        case 54: brainOfCthulhuAI(this, game, p); break;                  // AI_054 克脑(爬行者环→瞬移冲刺)
375	        case 55: creeperAI(this, game, p); break;                         // AI_055 爬行者(环绕漂移)
376	        case 30: twinsAI(this, game, p, false); break;                    // AI_030 雷神之眼 125(悬浮/连冲/变身/激光)
377	        case 31: twinsAI(this, game, p, true); break;                     // AI_031 魔神之眼 126(诅咒焰/火焰喷射)
378	        case 32: skeletronPrimeAI(this, game, p); break;                  // AI_032 机械骷髅王头 127(4 部件/旋冲/白天狂暴)
379	        case 33: case 34: case 35: case 36: primePartAI(this, game, p); break; // AI_033-036 Prime 部件(锯/激光/钳/炮)
380	        case 37: destroyerAI(this, game, p); break;                       // AI_037 毁灭者 134(80 段链穿地/探针激光)
381	        case 27: wallOfFleshAI(this, game, (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player ?? null); break; // AI_027 血肉墙(空腔扫描/速度血量档/Leech 轮)
382	        case 28: wofEyeAI(this, game, p); break;                          // AI_028 血肉墙之眼(钉墙吸附/激光连射 83)
383	        case 29: hungryAI(this, game, p); break;                          // AI_029 饥饿者(挂墙环绕→受击脱落扑咬)
384	        case 84: lunaticCultistAI(this, game, p); break;                  // AI_084 拜月教邪教徒(瞬移+四系弹幕轮换)
385	        case 120: empressOfLightAI(this, game, p); break;                 // AI_120 光之女皇(连段选招/半血变身)
386	        case 121: queenSlimeAI(this, game, p); break;                     // AI_121 史莱姆皇后(走路四连跳→飞行二阶段)
387	        case 100: ancientLightAI(this, game, p); break;                  // AI_100 远古之光 522(直飞投射物型)
388	        case 101: ancientDoomAI(this, game, p); break;                  // AI_101 远古末日 523(锚邪教徒蓄能自爆)
389	        case 43: queenBeeAI(this, game, p); break;                        // AI_043 蜂后(悬浮俯冲/蜜蜂机枪/毒刺)
390	        case 50: planteraHookAI(this, game, p); break;                    // AI_050 真菌孢子 261/孢子 265
391	        case 51: planteraAI(this, game, p); break;                        // AI_051 世纪之花(钩蔓牵引→二阶段)
392	        case 52: planteraTentacleAI(this, game, p); break;                // AI_052 钩蔓 263(抓墙换锚收线)
393	        case 53: planteraTentacle2AI(this, game, p); break;               // AI_053 触须 264(二阶段环绕)
394	        case 69: dukeFishronAI(this, game, p); break;                     // AI_069 猪鲨(冲撞/泡泡/龙卷,半血变身)
395	        case 70: dukeBubbleAI(this, game, p); break;                      // AI_070 爆裂泡泡 371
396	        case 71: sharkronAI(this, game, p); break;                        // AI_071 鲨鱼龙 372/373(龙卷出鲨,升空蓄力→扑咬)
397	        case 77: moonLordCoreAI(this, game, p); break;                    // AI_077 月总核心 398(双手死前无敌)
398	        case 78: moonLordHandAI(this, game, p); break;                    // AI_078 月总手 397(攻表/睁眼可击)
399	        case 79: moonLordHeadAI(this, game, p); break;                    // AI_079 月总头 396(死亡之光/吸血)
400	        case 45: golemAI(this, game, p); break;                        // AI_045 石巨人本体 245(蓄力跳/挂头无敌)
401	        case 46: golemHeadAI(this, game, p); break;                      // AI_046 挂载头 246(锚本体/火焰弹眼激光;249 自由态)
402	        case 47: golemFistAI(this, game, p); break;                      // AI_047 石巨人拳 247/248(肩锚冲拳)
403	        case 40: this.wallCreeperAI(game, p); break;                      // AI_040 爬墙蜘蛛族（贴背景墙爬行+形态互转）
404	        case 42: this.lostGirlAI(game, p); break;                         // AI_042 失落女孩（伪装静止→Nymph 变身 cs:30281-30310）
405	        case 0: this.boundNPCAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_000 被缚NPC（cs:19774 站立/水上漂流；Enemy 侧防御，常规走 TownNPC.bound）
406	        case 17: this.vultureAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_017 秃鹫（cs:24079 栖息→200px盒/受击起飞，单向不回落）
407	        case 19: this.antlionAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_019 蚁狮（cs:24465 扎沙不动+沙球；传送带特例略）
408	        case 23: this.flyingWeaponAI(game, p); break;                     // AI_023 飞行武器（cs:25316 冲/漂100/转120 循环，受击回蓄转）
409	        case 25: this.mimicAI(game, p); break;                            // AI_025 宝箱怪（cs:25621 伪装200px盒→小小大跳循环）

414	        case 26: this.chargerAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_026 独角兽/冲锋族（原版核）

416	        case 94: this.towerAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_094 星璇四塔（NPC.cs:41029-41443，核心 1:1）
417	        // ---- 星璇塔四组专属怪 AI 一期近似（docs/event-lunar-pillars.md §4）----
418	        // 74 穿心者（悬浮俯冲）→ AI_002 飘浮眼核；75 日耀骑手 → AI_003 战士核；
419	        // 85 星细胞/星云头蟹、95 小星细胞、96 水流入侵者、99 日耀耀斑（漂浮追击族）→ AI_005 蜂群核；

422	        case 75: this.fighterAI(game, p); break;
423	        case 85: case 95: case 96: case 99: this.swarmerAI(game, p); break;
424	        case 97: this.casterAI(game, p); break;

427	        // 对应 AI_0XX 1:1 补齐（docs/event-pumpkin-frost-moon.md 步骤 11）

430	    } else switch (this.key) {
431	      case 'slime_green':
432	      case 'slime_blue': this.slimeAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null); break;
433	      case 'zombie': this.zombieAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null); break;
434	      case 'demon_eye':
435	      case 'cave_bat': this.flyAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null, 0.09); break;
436	      case 'servant_of_cthulhu': this.flyAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null, 0.22); break;
437	      case 'eye_of_cthulhu': this.eocAI(game, player ?? null); break;

439	
440	    // 入水溅落声（与主角一致）：音量随与主角距离衰减，Boss 更响
441	    const eli = game.world.store.liquid[game.world.store.idx(
442	      Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE),
443	    )];
444	    const wasWater = this.inWater;
445	    this.inWater = eli > 80;
446	    if (this.inWater && !wasWater && hasPlayer) {
447	      const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
448	      const vol = Math.max(0.12, Math.min(1, 1 - d / (TILE * 45))) * (this.def.boss ? 1.2 : 0.9);
449	      game.playSfx('splash', vol);
450	    }
451	
452	    // 岩浆接触（NPC.cs:94520-94545）：30t 独立冷却（immune[255],不受普通受击 iframes 影响）,
453	    // onFire 7s + 50 直伤;lavaImmune 族（NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE,SetDefaults 提取）完全豁免
454	    if (this.vanillaId === null || !NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE.has(this.vanillaId)) {
455	      if (this.lavaCd > 0) this.lavaCd--;
456	      const inLava = liquidCollision(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, 2);
457	      if (inLava) {
458	        this.lavaWet = true;
459	        if (this.lavaCd <= 0) {
460	          this.lavaCd = 30;
461	          this.onFireT = Math.max(this.onFireT, 7); // AddBuff(24,420)
462	          this.hurt(50, 0, 0, game);                // StrikeNPCNoInteraction(50)
463	          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF9A3C', 10, 1.6, { life: 30, grav: -0.05 });
464	        }
465	      } else {
466	        this.lavaWet = false;
467	      }
468	    } else {
469	      this.lavaWet = false;
470	    }
471	    // 入水熄灭 onFire（NPC.cs:94284 TryRemovingWaterPerishableEffects:水湿(非岩浆)→DelBuff 24）
472	    if (this.inWater && !this.lavaWet) this.onFireT = 0;
473	    // onFire DoT（NPC.cs:92619:lifeRegen-8 = 4 HP/s,无防御结算）+ 火尘粒子（:92187）
474	    if (this.onFireT > 0) {
475	      this.onFireT -= dt;
476	      this.onFireDoT += 4 * dt;
477	      if (this.onFireDoT >= 1) {
478	        const n = Math.floor(this.onFireDoT);
479	        this.onFireDoT -= n;
480	        this.hp -= n;
481	        this.hpBarT = 240;
482	        if (this.hp <= 0) this.hurt(9999, 0, 0, game); // 走标准死亡管线(掉落/分裂/联机)
483	      }
484	      if (Math.random() < 0.75) {
485	        game.spawnParticles(
486	          this.x + Math.random() * this.w, this.y + Math.random() * this.h,
487	          Math.random() < 0.7 ? '#FF9A3C' : '#FFD24A', 1, 0.8,
488	          { life: 26 + Math.random() * 14, grav: -0.06, damp: 0.96 });
489	      }
490	    }
491	
492	    // ---- 武器浸剂 debuff 滴血（R2，NPC.cs:92585/92697/92708 DoT 同 onFire 模式） ----
493	    if (this.poisonT > 0) { this.poisonT -= dt; this.poisonDoT += 6 * dt; }
494	    if (this.venomT > 0) { this.venomT -= dt; this.venomDoT += 30 * dt; }
495	    if (this.cursedT > 0) { this.cursedT -= dt; this.cursedDoT += 24 * dt; }
496	    for (const [tKey, dKey] of [['poisonT', 'poisonDoT'], ['venomT', 'venomDoT'], ['cursedT', 'cursedDoT']] as const) {
497	      const acc = this[dKey];
498	      if (acc >= 1) {
499	        const n = Math.floor(acc);
500	        this[dKey] -= n;
501	        this.hp -= n;
502	        this.hpBarT = 240;
503	        if (this.hp <= 0) this.hurt(9999, 0, 0, game);
504	      }
505	    }
506	    if (this.ichorT > 0) this.ichorT -= dt;
507	    if (this.midasT > 0) this.midasT -= dt;
508	    if (this.confusedT > 0) {
509	      this.confusedT -= dt;
510	      // 纳米（confused 近似）：每 60t 翻转水平速度方向（原版 AI 反向 :29811）
511	      this.confuseFlip = (this.confuseFlip ?? 0) + dt;
512	      if (this.confuseFlip >= 1) { this.confuseFlip = 0; this.vx *= -1; }
513	    }
514	
515	    // 接触伤害（带上攻击者引用，供荆棘反伤）。damage=0（小动物等友好生物）不触发——
516	    // 否则 damagePlayer(0) 仍会击退/闪红，表现为"兔子撞我有攻击性"
517	    if (hasPlayer && this.aabbOverlaps(player) && this.def.damage > 0) {
518	      game.damagePlayer(this.def.damage, this.cx, this.cy, this);
519	    }
520	
521	    // Despawn 系统（原版 CheckActive, NPC.cs:78669-78798 + EncourageDespawn :7228-7235）：
522	    // timeLeft(activeTime=750) 屏内矩形每 tick 重置+清鼓励旗；离屏从当前值倒数，归零即消失。
523	    // EncourageDespawn(10) 封顶后离屏 ~10t 即消；屏内每 tick 被重置抵挡——
524	    // 僵尸/眼球白天游荡或上飞，一离屏就消失（原版天亮清夜怪的实际语义）。
525	    // Boss 默认豁免，但 despawnEncouraged（骷髅王离场/原版通用 EncourageDespawn）后
526	    // 同样倒数——且玩家死亡（player=null）时视为离屏（原版 CheckActive 不依赖玩家存活）
527	    if (this.vanillaId === 488 || (this.vanillaId != null && this.vanillaId in TOWER_KEY)) {
528	      // 训练假人不参与 despawn（原版 netAlways + TileEntity 生命周期由锚 tile 管理）；
529	      // 四塔同样豁免（DoesntDespawnToInactivity 名单 NPC.cs:78634-78635 含 422/493/507/517
530	      // ——事件建筑离屏消失会让 UpdateLunarApocalypse 永远等不到塔死）
531	    } else if (!this.def.boss || this.despawnEncouraged) {
532	      // 屏内判定跳过死亡玩家（原版 CheckActive 只用 active 玩家重置 timeLeft：
533	      // 尸体位置不算"屏内"——此前死亡玩家照常重置，Boss 鼓励消散全部失效）
534	      const pp = player && !player.dead ? player : null;
535	      const dxp = pp ? Math.abs(pp.cx - this.cx) : Infinity;
536	      const dyp = pp ? Math.abs(pp.cy - this.cy) : Infinity;
537	      // 屏内矩形（原版 rectangle2：sWidth/sHeight+2w 固定 1920×1080 → 半轴约 62×35 格）
538	      if (dxp < TILE * 62 && dyp < TILE * 35) {
539	        this.despawnTimer = 750;
540	        this.despawnEncouraged = false;
541	      } else {
542	        this.despawnTimer--;
543	        if (this.despawnTimer <= 0) {
544	          if (this.def.boss) this.bossFled = true; // 非击杀消亡:天亮离场/玩家死后撤离
545	          // 风气球 594 自然消散：挂载史莱姆一并消失（防 -999 冻结悬空）
546	          if (this.vanillaId === 594) {
547	            const sl = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.ai3 && n.ai0 === -999);
548	            if (sl) sl.dead = true;
549	          }
550	          this.dead = true;
551	        }
552	      }
553	    }
554	  }
555	
556	  /** 原版 EncourageDespawn(despawnTime)（NPC.cs:7228-7235）：timeLeft 封顶 + 置鼓励旗 */
557	  encourageDespawn(despawnTime: number): void {
558	    if (this.despawnTimer > despawnTime) this.despawnTimer = despawnTime;
559	    this.despawnEncouraged = true;
560	  }

562	  /** 敌怪弹幕发射辅助：从 (ox,oy) 朝 (tx,ty) 偏移方向以 speed 定速发射敌对 Dart */
563	  private shootDart(game: GameHooks, ox: number, oy: number, tx: number, ty: number, speed: number, dmg: number): void {
564	    const d = Math.hypot(tx, ty) || 1;
565	    (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities
566	      .add(new Dart(ox, oy, (tx / d) * speed, (ty / d) * speed, dmg), 'projectiles');
567	  }
568	
569	  private groundPhysics(game: GameHooks, jumpV = 0, accel = 0.12, maxSpd = 1, buoyant = false) {
570	    // 浮力生物（史莱姆）：水中上浮 + 阻尼，漂在水面而不是沉底
571	    if (buoyant) {
572	      const st = game.world.store;
573	      const li = st.liquid[st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))];
574	      if (li > 80) {
575	        if (this.state === 1) {
576	          // 跳跃中：弱重力助其跃出水面（浮力阻尼会把起跳速度吃掉导致困在水里）
577	          this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY * 0.3, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
578	          moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
579	          this.vx *= 0.98;
580	          return;
581	        }
582	        this.vy -= 0.32;
583	        this.vy *= 0.92;
584	        this.vx *= 0.95;
585	        moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
586	        return;
587	      }
588	    }
589	    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
590	    this.vx = Math.max(-maxSpd, Math.min(maxSpd, this.vx + accel * Math.sign(this.vx || 1) * 0));
591	    if (jumpV && this.onGround) this.vy = -jumpV;
592	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
593	    this.vx *= this.onGround ? 0.8 : 0.98;
594	  }
595	
596	  /** AI_001_Slimes 原版核（1.4.5.6 NPC.cs:60735+）：
597	   *  站定 vx*=0.8；ai[0] 每 tick +1（激愤 +1 额外）；

599	   *  -1000..-500 → 小跳（ai0=-2120）；-2000..-1500 → 大跳（vy=-8, vx+=3*dir, ai0=-200）。
600	   *  即：小跳→小跳→大跳循环，约 5-10 秒节奏。
```

```
98	  const st = game.world.store;
99	  const cols = [e.x + 2, e.cx, e.x + e.w - 2];
100	  if (e.vy >= 0) {

102	    let top = Infinity;

108	    else e.onGround = false;
109	  } else {
110	    e.onGround = false;
111	    const ty = Math.floor(e.y / TILE);
112	    let bottom = -Infinity;
113	    for (const cx of cols) {
114	      const tx = Math.floor(cx / TILE);
115	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) bottom = Math.max(bottom, (ty + 1) * TILE);
116	    }
117	    if (bottom > -Infinity) { e.y = bottom; e.vy = 0; }
118	  }
119	  const rows = [e.y + 2, e.cy, e.y + e.h - 2];
120	  if (e.vx > 0) {
121	    const tx = Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE);
122	    for (const ry of rows) {
123	      const ty = Math.floor(ry / TILE);
124	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { e.x = tx * TILE - e.w; e.vx = 0; break; }
125	    }
126	  } else if (e.vx < 0) {
127	    const tx = Math.floor(e.x / TILE);
128	    for (const ry of rows) {
129	      const ty = Math.floor(ry / TILE);
130	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { e.x = (tx + 1) * TILE; e.vx = 0; break; }
131	    }
132	  }
133	}

139	  const y0 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((e.y + e.h - 0.01) / TILE);
140	  for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {

143	    }
144	  }
145	  return false;
146	}

226	export function golemAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {

229	  // ---- 血尽转换(首行):死亡演出 60t 后真死;头由自身 AI 转自由态续战 ----

246	      p.x = e.cx + ox; p.y = e.cy + oy;
247	      p.ai0 = side;                // 拳 ai0=侧别(-1 左/1 右;头不使用)
248	    };
249	    spawn(247, -84, -9, -1);
250	    spawn(248, 78, -9, 1);
251	    spawn(246, -3, -57, 0);
252	  }
253	
254	  // ---- 出生渐显(NPC.cs:19477-19485):期间 ai[1]=0,蓄力冻结 ----
255	  if (fadeAlpha(e, x) > 0) e.ai1 = 0;
256	
257	  // ---- 死亡演出:无敌+碎裂尘,60t 后真死(头/拳继续) ----
258	  if (x.dying) {
259	    e.iframes = 2;
260	    e.vx *= 0.9;
261	    e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAV, MAX_FALL);
262	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
263	    collideGround(e, game);
264	    x.deadT++;
265	    if (x.deadT % 6 === 0) {
266	      game.spawnParticles(
267	        e.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * e.w, e.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * e.h,
268	        '#B04030', 6, 2.4, { life: 36 });
269	    }
270	    if (x.deadT >= 60) { e.dead = true; return; }
271	    return;
272	  }
273	
274	  const num = balanceNum(game, player);
275	
276	  // ---- 部件存活扫描(NPC.cs:19487-19512):flag=头/flag2=左拳/flag3=右拳 ----
277	  //  注意头原体变身自由态后(ai3=1)视为"挂载头已失"——本体解除无敌且蓄力提速,
278	  //  对齐原版"246 死亡 → NewNPC 249"后 flag 为假的语义
279	  let hasHead = false, hasL = false, hasR = false;
280	  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
281	    if (n.dead) continue;
282	    if (n.vanillaId === 246 && n.ai3 !== 1) hasHead = true;
283	    else if (n.vanillaId === 247) hasL = true;
284	    else if (n.vanillaId === 248) hasR = true;
285	  }
286	  // dontTakeDamage = 挂载头在场(NPC.cs:19513):一阶段本体无敌,先打头/拳
287	  if (hasHead) e.iframes = 2;
288	  // getGoodWorld 落体砸火把(:19514-19534,expert 略)
289	
290	  // ---- 断臂尘(NPC.cs:19536-19595):原版每 tick 双簇 Dust+Gore,此处每 10t 一簇粒子近似 ----
291	  if (!hasL && e.aiT % 10 === 0) game.spawnParticles(e.cx - 80 * SCALE, e.cy - 9, '#9A8FA0', 3, 1.2, { life: 40 });
292	  if (!hasR && e.aiT % 10 === 0) game.spawnParticles(e.cx + 62 * SCALE, e.cy - 9, '#9A8FA0', 3, 1.2, { life: 40 });
293	
294	  // ---- 跳跃穿墙解除(NPC.cs:19569-19578):下落低于玩家顶 / 有视线且自身不在块内 ----
295	  if (x.noTC && player && !player.dead) {
296	    if (e.vy > 0 && e.y + e.h > player.y) x.noTC = false;
297	    else if (losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy) && !hitSolid(game, e)) x.noTC = false;
298	  }
299	
300	  // TargetClosest 的 direction 近似:朝玩家符号,同侧回退 facing
301	  const dir = player ? (Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || e.facing || 1) : 1;
302	  if (player) e.facing = dir;
303	
304	  if (player && e.ai0 === 0) {
305	    // ---- 地面蓄力(NPC.cs:19580-19645):ai[1] 攒 300 → -20 蹲伏 20t → ==-1 起跳 ----
306	    //  蹲伏段(负值)num4 固定 1;蓄力段按断臂/残血提速,再乘脱庙狂暴系数
307	    if (e.vy === 0) {
308	      e.vx *= 0.8;
309	      let num4 = 1;
310	      if (e.ai1 > 0) {
311	        if (!hasL) num4 += 2;
312	        if (!hasR) num4 += 2;
313	        if (!hasHead) num4 += 2;
314	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp) num4 += 1;
315	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num4 += 4;
316	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 3) num4 += 8;
317	        num4 *= num;
318	        // getGoodWorld:num4 += 100(:19613-19616,expert 略)
319	      }
320	      e.ai1 += num4;
321	      if (e.ai1 >= 300) e.ai1 = -20;
322	      else if (e.ai1 === -1) {
323	        // 起跳(:19624-19643):穿墙跳,vx=4*dir;残血跳更高 -12.1*(num+9)/10 钳 -19.1
324	        x.noTC = true;
325	        e.vx = 4 * dir;
326	        e.vy = e.hp < e.maxHp ? Math.max(-19.1, (-12.1 * (num + 9)) / 10) : -12.1;
327	        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
328	      }
329	    }
330	  } else if (player && e.ai0 === 1) {
331	    // ---- 跳跃段(NPC.cs:19646-19708) ----
332	    if (e.vy === 0) {
333	      // 落地(:19648-19661):Item14 音效 + 落地尘/Gore
334	      game.playSfx('roar', 0.35);
335	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#9A8FA0', 12, 2.2, { life: 40 });
336	      e.ai0 = 0;
337	    } else {
338	      // 玩家在身下水平跨度内 → 收横向速度(夹住玩家下压),否则朝玩家加速钳 ±num7
339	      if (e.x < player.x + player.w && e.x + e.w > player.x) {
340	        e.vx *= 0.9;
341	        if (e.y + e.h < player.y) e.vy += (0.2 * (num + 1)) / 2;
342	      } else {
343	        e.vx += 0.2 * dir;
344	        let num7 = 3;
345	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp) num7 += 1;
346	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num7 += 1;
347	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num7 += 1;
348	        num7 *= (num + 1) / 2;
349	        e.vx = clamp(e.vx, -num7, num7);
350	      }
351	    }
352	  }
353	
354	  // ---- 玩家死亡 vs 远离（NPC.cs:19469-19475 / 19709-19721）----
355	  // 玩家死：仅 noTileCollide=true（无 despawn 无状态切换，AI 继续跑）；
356	  // 活人曼哈顿 >3000：瞬消（二次判定 TargetClosest 后）
357	  if (!player) {
358	    x.noTC = true;
359	  } else if (Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) + Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 3000) {
360	    e.dead = true; e.bossFled = true;
361	    return;
362	  }
363	
364	  // ---- 重力+位移+落地(原版共享物理:noGravity=false → 0.3/2t、maxFall 10) ----
365	  e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAV, MAX_FALL);
366	  e.x += e.vx;
367	  e.y += e.vy;
368	  if (!x.noTC) collideGround(e, game);
369	  else e.onGround = false;
370	}
371	
372	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
373	// aiStyle 46 挂载头 246(NPC.cs:31273-31499)+ aiStyle 48 自由头 249(NPC.cs:31500-31741)
374	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
375	
376	export function golemHeadAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
377	  const x = headXOf(e);
378	  if (e.vanillaId === 249) x.free = true;   // 直接生成的自由头(原版 NewNPC 249)
379	  if (!e.bInit) {
380	    e.bInit = true;
381	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
382	    e.ai3 = x.free ? 1 : 0;
383	    e.alphaFade = false;
384	    x.alpha = x.free ? 0 : 255;             // 249 SetDefaults 无 alpha(:12139-12149)
385	  }
386	
387	  // ---- 血尽转换(首行):自由头死=战斗结束全族自毁;挂载头死=转自由态 ----
388	  if (e.hp <= 0) {
389	    if (x.free) {
390	      e.dead = true;
391	      for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
392	        if (!n.dead && n !== e && n.vanillaId !== null && n.vanillaId >= 245 && n.vanillaId <= 249) n.dead = true;
393	      }
394	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#C04030', 20, 3, { life: 40 });
395	      game.playSfx('roar', 1);
396	      return;
397	    }
398	    golemHeadGoFree(e, game);
399	  }
400	
401	  // ---- 属性:自由头按 249 SetDefaults(NPC.cs:12139-12149:damage 80/defense 32) ----
402	  if (x.free) {
403	    const free = vanillaNpc(249);
404	    e.def.damage = free?.damage ?? 80;
405	    e.def.defense = free?.defense ?? 32;
406	  } else {
407	    const b = baseOf(e);
408	    e.def.damage = b.dmg;
409	    e.def.defense = b.def;
410	  }
411	
412	  // 本体死亡/缺失 → 挂载头转自由态(本体死透后头继续)
413	  const body = findBody(game);
414	  if (!x.free && (!body || body.ai3 === 1 || body.hp <= 0)) golemHeadGoFree(e, game);
415	
416	  const num = balanceNum(game, player);
417	
418	  if (!x.free && body) {
419	    // ================= 挂载态 aiStyle 46(NPC.cs:31273-31499) =================
420	    // noTileCollide = true(:31284);锚点 = 本体.Center + (-3*scale,-57*scale)(:31290-31308)
421	    const dx = body.cx - 3 * SCALE - e.cx;
422	    const dy = body.cy - 57 * SCALE - e.cy;
423	    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
424	    if (dist < 100) { e.spin = 0; e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy; }        // 100 内整帧贴锚
425	    else { e.vx = (dx / dist) * 100; e.vy = (dy / dist) * 100; e.spin = e.vx * 0.1; }
426	    // 渐显(:31310-31317):期间 ai[1]=30
427	    if (fadeAlpha(e, x) > 0) e.ai1 = 30;
428	
429	    if (player) {
430	      if (e.ai0 === 0) {
431	        // ---- 嘴部火焰弹循环(:31319-31347):300t 一发 proj 258(伤 18 速 8) ----
432	        e.ai1 += 1;
433	        if (e.ai1 < 20 || e.ai1 > 280) { e.ai1 += (2 * (num - 1)) / 3; x.l0 = 1; }  // 张嘴帧
434	        else { e.ai1 += (1 * (num - 1)) / 2; x.l0 = 0; }
435	        if (e.ai1 >= 300) {
436	          e.ai1 = 0;
437	          golemSpit(game, e, player, 0, 10 * SCALE, 8, 18, 258);
438	        }
439	      } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {
440	        // ---- 眼部阶段(:31353-31486):嘴弹伤 24 + 眼激光 259(伤 28) ----
441	        x.l1 = player.cx < e.cx - e.w ? -1 : player.cx > e.cx + e.w ? 1 : 0;        // localAI[1]
442	        const num733 = (num + 3) / 4;
443	        e.ai1 += num733;
444	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.4) e.ai1 += num733;
445	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.2) e.ai1 += num733;
446	        x.l0 = e.ai1 < 20 || e.ai1 > 280 ? 1 : 0;
447	        if (e.ai1 >= 300) {
448	          e.ai1 = 0;
449	          golemSpit(game, e, player, x.l1 * 40 * SCALE, 10 * SCALE, 8, 24, 258);
450	        }
451	        e.ai2 += num733;
452	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 3) e.ai2 += num733;
453	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) e.ai2 += num733;
454	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 5) e.ai2 += num733;
455	        if (!losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) e.ai2 += 4;          // :31456 无视线催速
456	        if (e.ai2 > 60 + Math.random() * 600) {
457	          e.ai2 = 0;
458	          golemEyeLasers(game, e, player, x.l1);
459	        }
460	      }
461	      // 阶段派生(:31487-31491):血 < 半 → 眼部阶段
462	      e.ai0 = e.hp < e.maxHp / 2 ? 1 : 0;
463	    }
464	  } else {
465	    // ================= 自由态 aiStyle 48 GolemHeadFree(NPC.cs:31500-31741) =================
466	    // flag48(:31503-31515):头自身看不到玩家 → 穿墙 + 火焰弹不蓄满(头↔玩家视线)
467	    const rBody = findBody(game);
468	    const noLos = !player || !losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy);
469	    // flag49(:31646-31651):本体↔玩家无视线 → 激光催速 + 弹道预判;
470	    // 本体已亡(本移植死亡链)→ 退化为头↔玩家视线
471	    const noLosBody = rBody
472	      ? !player || !losClear(game, rBody.cx, rBody.cy, player.cx, player.cy)
473	      : noLos;
474	    if (player) {
475	      // 悬浮(:31521-31564):目标 = 玩家 + (0,-300),7 速/0.05 步进(符号翻转双步进)
476	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - 300 - e.cy;
477	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
478	      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * 7, 0.05);
479	      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * 7, 0.05);
480	      // 火焰弹节奏(:31566-31595):num761=(num+4)/5,分档读本体(golemBoss=245)残血(:31574-31594)。
481	      // 本体已亡(本移植死亡链,原版无此态)→ 比值 0 = 全档,等价原版读尸体槽 life<=0 恒真
482	      const num761 = (num + 4) / 5;
483	      e.ai1 += num761;
484	      const r = rBody ? rBody.hp / rBody.maxHp : 0;
485	      if (r < 0.8) e.ai1 += num761;
486	      if (r < 0.6) e.ai1 += num761;
487	      if (r < 0.2) e.ai1 += num761;
488	      if (r < 0.1) e.ai1 += num761;
489	      x.l0 = e.ai1 < 20 || e.ai1 > 280 ? 1 : 0;   // 张嘴帧(:31584-31592)
490	      if (noLos) e.ai1 = 20;                       // :31593-31595 无视线时不蓄满
491	      if (e.ai1 >= 300) {                          // :31596-31617 嘴弹 proj 258(伤 20 速 8)
492	        e.ai1 = 0;
493	        golemSpit(game, e, player, 0, -10 * SCALE, 8, 20, 258);
494	      }
495	      // 眼激光节奏(:31618-31658):基数 num,分档读本体残血(:31626-31645;r 与上方火焰弹
496	      // 同源);激光伤/速分档(:31683-31705,阈值 0.5/0.4/0.3/0.2/0.1)经 golemEyeLasersFree 传 r
497	      e.ai2 += num;
498	      if (r < 1 / 1.25) e.ai2 += num;
499	      if (r < 1 / 1.5) e.ai2 += num;
500	      if (r < 1 / 2) e.ai2 += num;
501	      if (r < 1 / 3) e.ai2 += num;
502	      if (r < 1 / 4) e.ai2 += num;
503	      if (r < 1 / 5) e.ai2 += num;
504	      if (r < 1 / 6) e.ai2 += num;
505	      if (noLosBody) e.ai2 += num * 10;            // :31646-31651 flag49 催速
506	      if (e.ai2 > 100 + Math.random() * 4800) {
507	        e.ai2 = 0;
508	        golemEyeLasersFree(game, e, player, noLosBody, r);
509	      }
510	    }
511	    // :31721-31740 排焰尘(纯视觉,略)
512	  }
513	
514	  e.x += e.vx;
515	  e.y += e.vy;
516	}
517	
518	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
519	// AI_047 石巨人拳 247/248(NPC.cs:19171-19446)
520	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
521	// 原版 ai[0] 是状态机(0 归位/1 蓄力/2 冲拳/3 直飞);任务约定 ai0=侧别,
522	// 状态机落在本文件 WeakMap(fistX.state),计时仍用 ai[1]。
523	
524	export function golemFistAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
525	  const x = fistXOf(e);
526	  if (!e.bInit) {
527	    e.bInit = true;
528	    e.ai0 = e.vanillaId === 247 ? -1 : 1;   // ai0=侧别(-1 左拳/1 右拳;由本体生成时预置)
529	    e.ai1 = 0;
530	    e.alphaFade = false;
531	    x.alpha = 255;
532	  }
533	  const isLeft = e.vanillaId === 247;
534	
535	  // ---- 锚主:golemBoss 缺失 → 自毁(NPC.cs:19182-19186) ----
536	  //  本体存活(含死亡演出)→ 锚本体;本体死透 → 锚自由头(任务约定"锚定本体(或头)")
537	  const body = findBody(game);
538	  const head = findHead(game);
539	  const anchor = body && !body.dead ? body : head;
540	  if (!anchor) { e.dead = true; return; }
541	  e.master = anchor; // 拳链渲染用（Renderer.drawGolemFistChain，Main.cs:22593-22632）
542	
543	  const num = balanceNum(game, player);
544	  // 渐显(:19187-19194):期间 ai[1]=0
545	  if (fadeAlpha(e, x) > 0) e.ai1 = 0;
546	
547	  // ---- 肩锚点(:19197-19199):锚主.Center + 锚主.velocity + (±84/78*scale, -9*scale) ----
548	  const offX = isLeft ? -84 : 78;
549	  const tx = anchor.cx + anchor.vx + offX * SCALE;
550	  const ty = anchor.cy + anchor.vy - 9 * SCALE;
551	  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
552	  const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
553	  e.facing = isLeft ? 1 : -1;               // 镜像朝向(原版贴图方向)
554	
555	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
556	
557	  if (x.state === 0) {
558	    // ---- 归位/待机蓄力(:19202-19270) ----
559	    x.noTC = true;
560	    let num3 = 14;
561	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num3 += 3;
562	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num3 += 3;
563	    if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) num3 += 8;
564	    num3 *= (num + 3) / 4;
565	    if (num3 > 32) num3 = 32;
566	    if (dist < 12 + num3) {
567	      // 贴肩:整帧速度 = 偏移量(原版 velocity = delta),蓄力计时(:19216-19259)
568	      e.spin = 0;
569	      e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy;
570	      e.ai1 += num;
571	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) e.ai1 += num;
572	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) e.ai1 += num;
573	      if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) e.ai1 += 10 * num;
574	      if (e.ai1 >= 60) {
575	        // 出拳侧别门(:19244-19256):左拳只在玩家偏左时出、右拳对称
576	        const sideOk = isLeft ? e.cx + 100 > player.cx : e.cx - 100 < player.cx;
577	        e.ai1 = 0;
578	        if (sideOk) x.state = 1;
579	      }
580	    } else {
581	      e.vx = (dx / dist) * num3;
582	      e.vy = (dy / dist) * num3;
583	      // 归位朝向(:19260-19262):默认 atan2(-vy,-vx),247 镜像为 atan2(vy,vx)
584	      e.spin = isLeft ? Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) : Math.atan2(-e.vy, -e.vx);
585	    }
586	  } else if (x.state === 1) {
587	    // ---- 蓄力:贴肩 30t(:19271-19332) ----
588	    e.ai1 += 1;
589	    e.x = tx - e.w / 2;                     // 原版 base.Center = vector(整帧钉在肩锚)
590	    e.y = ty - e.h / 2;
591	    e.spin = 0; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
592	    if (e.ai1 <= 15) {                      // :19278-19292 聚能尘(粒子近似)
593	      game.spawnParticles(
594	        e.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30, e.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30,
595	        '#C8A040', 1, 0.8, { life: 26 });
596	    }
597	    if (e.ai1 >= 30) {
598	      x.noTC = true;
599	      x.state = 2; e.ai1 = 0;
600	      // 冲拳速度(:19302-19321):12 + 残血/本体受损加成,×(num+3)/4 钳 48
601	      let num6 = 12;
602	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num6 += 4;
603	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num6 += 4;
604	      if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) num6 += 10;
605	      num6 *= (num + 3) / 4;
606	      if (num6 > 48) num6 = 48;
607	      const pdx = player.cx - e.cx, pdy = player.cy - e.cy;
608	      const pd = Math.hypot(pdx, pdy) || 1;
609	      e.vx = (pdx / pd) * num6; e.vy = (pdy / pd) * num6;
610	      // 冲拳朝向(:19325-19328):默认 atan2(vy,vx),247 镜像为 atan2(-vy,-vx)(与归位相反)
611	      e.spin = isLeft ? Math.atan2(-e.vy, -e.vx) : Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
612	    }
613	  } else if (x.state === 2) {
614	    // ---- 冲拳飞行(:19333-19393) ----
615	    e.ai1 += 1;
```

```
// Boss AI 1:1 移植(Terarria1456 NPC.cs SetDefaults 指定 aiStyle 的专用核)。
// 由 Enemy.fixedUpdate 的 aiStyle 分发调用;弹幕用 MagicProj/投射桶,部件用 fromVanilla。
// 各核保留原版状态机主链与特征机制(移速/计时/阶段),专家专属分支注释标明。
import type { Enemy } from './Enemy';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Player } from './Player';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import { MagicProj } from './MagicProj';
import { Arrow } from './Arrow';

const addProj = (game: GameHooks, p: MagicProj) => {
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
};
export const addEnemy = (game: GameHooks, e: Enemy) => {
  const em = (game as unknown as { entities?: { nextId?: number; add?: (en: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;
  if (em?.nextId !== undefined) e.id = em.nextId++;
  em?.add?.(e, 'enemies');
  return e;
};

 *  悬浮: Y 拉向玩家上方 250(0.02/±2),X 朝玩家 0.05/±8;旋冲: rotation+=0.3*dir、
 *  朝玩家 1.5 倍速、伤害 ×1.3、防御 -10(:22026-22179)。 */
export function skeletronBossAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  const isGuardian = e.vanillaId === 68;
  // 初始化:生成双手 36(:21914-21931)
  if (!e.bInit) {
    e.bInit = true;
    if (!isGuardian) {
....
  e.vx += (tx - e.x) * 0.01;
  e.vy += (ty - e.y) * 0.01;
  e.vx *= 0.92; e.vy *= 0.92;
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
  void game;
}

// ================= 机械三王(NPC.cs AI_030-037,1.4.5.6 行号见各核注释) =================
// 经典模式参数 1:1;专家/大师/FTW 分支仅注释保留。

/** 原版速度逼近(L26539-26670 雏形):朝目标速度单轴步进,符号翻转时双倍步进 */
export function accelTo(v: number, target: number, a: number): number {
  if (v < target) { v += a; if (v < 0 && target > 0) v += a; }
  else if (v > target) { v -= a; if (v > 0 && target < 0) v -= a; }
  return v;
}
/** 朝 (tx,ty) 以速度 spd/加速度 acc 追赶(vanilla numXXX = spd/dist 归一后 accelTo) */
export function seek(e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, acc: number): void {
  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * spd, acc);
  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * spd, acc);
}
/** 直线视线(原版 Collision.CanHit 近似:8px 采样无实心块) */
export function losClear(game: GameHooks, x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number): boolean {
  const st = game.world.store;
  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) / 8));
  for (let i = 1; i < steps; i++) {
    const t = i / steps;
    const tx = Math.floor((x0 + (x1 - x0) * t) / TILE), ty = Math.floor((y0 + (y1 - y0) * t) / TILE);
    if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return false;
  }
  return true;
}
/** Boss 弹幕:直飞(grav 0)+ 原版 Projectile_N 贴图 */
export function shoot(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, projId: number, life = 300): void {
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
    new Arrow(x, y, vx, vy, dmg, 0, projId, null, { grav: 0, life, pierce: 1 }), 'projectiles');
}

/** AI_030/031 双子(NPC.cs:26497-27735):雷神之眼 125 / 魔神之眼 126。
 *  旋转持续瞄准玩家(0.1/0.15 步进+绕圈处理);白天/玩家死 → 上飞离场;
 *  一阶段(血>40%):侧向悬浮(300/400)→4-10 连冲(12/13 速);二阶段变身自旋;
 *  三阶段:雷眼 激光 83→100(伤害×1.5 防+10),魔眼 火焰喷射 101(伤害×1.5 防+18)。 */
export function twinsAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null, spaz: boolean) {
  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
  // 白天/玩家死:上飞离场(原版 :26585/:27208 vy-=0.04 + EncourageDespawn(10))——
  // 玩家死亡派发为 null,一并进离场（此前 null 仅漂移不消散）
  if (game.world.clock.isDay || !player || player.dead) {
    e.vy -= 0.04;
    e.encourageDespawn(10); // 离屏即消(Enemy 打 bossFled,Game 不播击败/不记账)
    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
    return;
  }
  // ---- 旋转瞄准(num427/num471:atan2(dy-59, 自-玩)+π/2,步进 0.1/0.15) ----
  let target = Math.atan2(e.y + e.h - 59 - (player.y + player.h / 2), e.cx - player.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
  if (target < 0) target += 6.283; else if (target > 6.283) target -= 6.283;
  const step = spaz ? 0.15 : 0.1;
  let rot = e.spin;
  if (rot < target) { rot += (target - rot > Math.PI ? -step : step); }
  else if (rot > target) { rot += (rot - target > Math.PI ? step : -step); }
```

```
	public void SetDefaults(int Type, NPCSpawnParams spawnparams = default(NPCSpawnParams))
	{
		if (Main.getGoodWorld && !IsABestiaryIconDummy && spawnparams.sizeScaleOverride.HasValue)
		{
			spawnparams.sizeScaleOverride = (spawnparams.sizeScaleOverride + spawnparams.sizeScaleOverride * spawnparams.sizeScaleOverride) / 2f;
		}
		if (Type < 0)
		{
			SetDefaultsFromNetId(Type, spawnparams);
			return;
		}
		waterMovementSpeed = (lavaMovementSpeed = 0.5f);
		honeyMovementSpeed = 0.25f;
		altTexture = 0;
		townNpcVariationIndex = 0;
		nameOver = 0f;
		takenDamageMultiplier = 1f;
		rarity = 0;
		dontCountMe = false;
		releaseOwner = 255;
		catchItem = 0;
		needsUniqueInfoUpdate = true;
		netID = 0;
		netAlways = false;
		CanBeReplacedByOtherNPCs = false;
		statsAreScaledForThisManyPlayers = 0;
		difficulty = 1f;
		int num = 10;
		if (Type >= 0)
		{
			num = NPCID.Sets.TrailCacheLength[Type];
		}
		if (num != oldPos.Length)
		{
			Array.Resize(ref oldPos, num);
			Array.Resize(ref oldRot, num);
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < oldPos.Length; i++)
		{
			oldRot[i] = 0f;
			oldPos[i].X = 0f;
			oldPos[i].Y = 0f;
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < BuffID.Count; j++)
		{
			buffImmune[j] = false;
		}
		setFrameSize = false;
		spawnNeedsSyncing = false;
		realLife = -1;
		lifeRegen = 0;
		lifeRegenExpectedLossPerSecond = -1;
```

```
// 原版 1.4.0.5 全量 NPC 数据（tools/extract-npcs.mjs 从 Terarria1405/NPC.cs SetDefaults 提取）。
// 增量补齐（2026-08-11）：473-476/590/591/594/628/629/631/634/635/692 十三条取自
// Terarria1456/NPC.cs SetDefaults + Main.cs npcFrameCount（数值一律取 1456 最终态）。
// 星璇塔增量（2026-08-11）：403/404（星尘蠕虫身/尾，字段复制 402 改宽高——1456 无独立
// SetDefaults，原版由 DrawNPC case 402 自绘 trail；本仓蠕虫链 spawnWormChain 需要
// 头+1/头+2 条目）、408（小水流入侵者，复制 407 缩小——1456 无独立 SetDefaults）。
// aiStyle 与 AI_XXX 方法的对应见 NPC.cs AI() 分发；此处先覆盖高频家族，
// 未移植 AI 家族的 aiStyle 回退到最接近的已移植行为（注释标注）。
import vanillaNpcsJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json';
import vanillaDataJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';
import { TILE_DEFS } from './tiles';

/** tile id → key 反查（biomeAt 群系判定用） */
const TILE_KEY_NAME: string[] = TILE_DEFS.map((d) => d.key);

// （旧掉落表已退役：掉落走 src/drops/NpcDrops.ts 规则树——
//  tools/extract-npcdrops.mjs 结构化提取 ItemDropDatabase.cs，含专家/大师/条件/链语义）

/** 原版物品 id → 本仓库 item key（vanilla.json 的 key 是 PascalCase，ITEM_BY_KEY 多为 snake_case；
 *  未注册的返回 null 跳过） */
export const vanillaItemKey = (() => {
  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();
  const items = (vanillaDataJson as unknown as { items: Record<string, { key?: string }> }).items ?? {};
  return (itemId: number): string | null => {
    if (map.has(itemId)) return map.get(itemId)!;
    const meta = items[String(itemId)];
    let key: string | null = null;
    if (meta?.key) {
      const snake = meta.key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase();
      if (ITEM_BY_KEY[snake] != null) key = snake;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[meta.key] != null) key = meta.key;
      // vi_NNN 导入物品（items.ts 的全量原版物品命名）：vi_<id> 或 vi_<id>_<snake>
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}`;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;
    }
    map.set(itemId, key);
    return key;
  };
})();

10:import vanillaDataJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
11:import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';
12:import { TILE_DEFS } from './tiles';
```

```
3582	      game = dmg as unknown as GameHooks;
3583	      dmg = 5; kbx = 0; kby = 0;
3584	    }
3585	    const rawDmg = dmg; // 傀儡上报用原始值（下方 def 凘认算只做一次，双端一致）
3586	    if (this.iframes > 0) return false;
3587	    this.justHitT = 2; // 原版 justHit 单帧旗标（AI_023 飞行武器/陆龟受击回态消费）
3588	    // 训练假人（NPC 488，cs:83498 localAI 赋值）：immortal 永不死，

3590	    if (this.vanillaId === 488) {
3591	      this.dummyShake = Math.max(20, Math.min(120, dmg));
3592	      this.dummyHitDir = Math.sign(kbx) || this.dummyHitDir;
3593	      this.iframes = 8;

3600	    dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - effDef * 0.5));
```

```
// 蜂后 222(aiStyle 43,NPC.cs:30312-30998)+ 世纪之花族 1:1 移植(Terarria1456,1.4.5.6)。
//   aiStyle 50(NPC.cs:31807-31872)= NPC 261 真菌孢子 / 265 孢子(漂移族,261 撞墙即碎)
//   aiStyle 51(NPC.cs:31873-32237)= NPC 262 世纪之花本体(钩蔓均值牵引 + 二阶段触须)
//   aiStyle 52(NPC.cs:32238-32405)= NPC 263 世纪之花钩蔓(抓墙锚定,牵引本体)
// 经典模式参数 1:1;expert/getGoodWorld 分支只注释保留;netcode(netUpdate/Main.netMode)跳过。
// 本体/部件均 noTileCollide(原版 SetDefaults),故位移由各核自行积分(e.x+=vx)。
import type { Enemy } from './Enemy';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Player } from './Player';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import { moveAndCollide } from '../physics/TileCollision';
import { spawnPart, shoot, accelTo, losClear } from './bossAI';

/** 原版 localAI[0..3](Enemy 的 ai0-ai3 对应 ai[],localAI 是独立数组 → WeakMap 落盘) */
const localAI = new WeakMap<Enemy, number[]>();
const la = (e: Enemy): number[] => {
  let a = localAI.get(e);
  if (!a) { a = [0, 0, 0, 0]; localAI.set(e, a); }
  return a;
};

/** Main.rand.Next(n) → [0,n) */
const rnd = (n: number): number => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);
/** Main.rand.Next(a, b) → [a,b) */
const rndRange = (a: number, b: number): number => a + Math.floor(Math.random() * (b - a));
const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number): number => (v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v);
/** 蜂后毒刺态悬浮步进(NPC.cs:30960-30977):与 accelTo 同构,但符号翻转时补步 ×2 */
const step2 = (v: number, target: number, a: number): number => {
  if (v < target) { v += a; if (v < 0 && target > 0) v += a * 2; }
  else if (v > target) { v -= a; if (v > 0 && target < 0) v -= a * 2; }
  return v;
};

// ============================================================================
// AI_043 蜂后 222(NPC.cs:30312-30998)
// ai[0] 状态:-1 选下一招 / 0 悬浮-俯冲循环 / 2 飞至玩家上方 / 1 机枪蜜蜂 /
//            3 毒刺弹幕 / 4 远距追赶 / 5 玩家死离场。
// ai[1]:状态 0 内的半程计数(偶=悬浮 奇=冲撞减速,>2*num662 换招);离开时暂存上一状态供 -1 去重。
// ai[2]:状态 0 减速门(1=正在减速);状态 1/3 已发弹计数。
// ============================================================================
export function queenBeeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  // 首帧复位(ai0 默认 -1120 是史莱姆族约定,原版 ai[0] 从 0 起步)
  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; la(e)[0] = 0; }
  // 玩家失效 = 原版 TargetClosest 落空后的 dead4(:30316-30319)
  const dead4 = !player || player.dead;
  // 暴怒度 num658(:30321-30327):地表 +1 / 非丛林 +1(getGoodWorld +0.5 省略)。
  // ZoneJungle = Game.scene 窗口计数(丛林砖 60/61/62/74/226/225 ≥140 且非地狱层)
  let num658 = 0;
  if (e.y / TILE < game.world.groundLevel) num658 += 1;
  if (!game.scene?.zoneJungle) num658 += 1;
  const num659 = player ? Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) : 1e9;
  // timeLeft 维持 ≥60(:30330-30334)由共享 Despawn 系统承担,不在此重复
  if (e.ai0 !== 5 && num659 > 3000) e.ai0 = 4;   // :30336-30341 远距追赶
  if (dead4) e.ai0 = 5;                           // :30342-30345

  let direction = e.facing || 1;
  if (e.ai0 === 5) {
    // 离场(:30346-30383):朝世界远端飞,vx 恒向远端加速 0.08
    e.vy *= 0.98;
    direction = e.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1;
    e.facing = direction;
    const halfW = game.world.store.w * TILE / 2;   // Main.maxTilesX * 8 = 世界中线
    if (e.x < halfW) {
      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98; else la(e)[0] = 1;
      e.vx -= 0.08;
    } else {
      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98; else la(e)[0] = 1;
      e.vx += 0.08;
    }
    e.encourageDespawn(10); // :30390 离场态每 tick EncourageDespawn(10)——离屏 ~10t 消散
  } else if (e.ai0 === -1) {
    // 选下一招(:30384-30408):{0,2,3} 随机,避开 ai1 暂存的上一状态
    let pick: number;
    do {
      pick = rnd(3);
      if (pick === 1) pick = 2; else if (pick === 2) pick = 3;
    } while (pick === e.ai1);
    e.ai0 = pick;
    e.ai1 = 0;
    e.ai2 = 0;

```

```
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';

// AI 家族错误回退消除回归（aiStyle 0/17/19/23/25/39/41/44/56）
type DartLike = { vx: number; vy: number; damage: number };

function flatWorld(playerAt: { x: number; y: number }) {
  const w = new World(80, 80, 42, 'open');
  const st = w.store;
  for (let x = 10; x <= 70; x++) st.setTileSilent(x, 60, 2); // 地板 y=60
  const shots: DartLike[] = [];
  const hooks = {
    world: w,
    player: {
      x: playerAt.x * 16, y: playerAt.y * 16, w: 20, h: 42,
      cx: playerAt.x * 16, cy: playerAt.y * 16 + 21,
      dead: false, useTime: 0,
    },
    playSfx: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, spawnParticles: () => {},
    spawnDrop: () => null, damagePlayer: () => {},
    entities: { add: (e: unknown) => { shots.push(e as DartLike); } },
  } as never;
  return { w, hooks, shots };
}

describe('AI 家族回退消除', () => {
  it('被缚NPC 105（aiStyle 0）：原地站立不动、零接触伤害', () => {
    const { hooks } = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 58 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(105, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    expect(e.def.damage).toBe(0);          // friendly 旗 → 零伤害
    const x0 = Math.round(e.x);
    for (let t = 0; t < 120; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(Math.abs(e.x - x0)).toBeLessThan(1); // 不游走
  });

  it('秃鹫 61（aiStyle 17）：栖息不动；玩家进入 200px 盒 → 起飞（vy=-6）', () => {
    const far = flatWorld({ x: 60, y: 40 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(61, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    e.y = 60 * 16 - e.h; e.vy = 0;   // 贴地栖息（悬空出生原版语义=直接起飞）
    for (let t = 0; t < 60; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, far.hooks);
    expect(e.ai0).toBe(0);                  // 远处保持栖息
    const near = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 58 });
    const e2 = Enemy.fromVanilla(61, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    let tookOff = false;
    for (let t = 0; t < 30; t++) {
      e2.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, near.hooks);
      if (e2.ai0 === 1) { tookOff = true; break; }
    }
    expect(tookOff).toBe(true);
  });

  it('蚁狮 69（aiStyle 19）：扎地不动；玩家在上方 200t 后射沙球（速 12、伤 10）', () => {
    const { hooks, shots } = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 55 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(69, 40 * 16, 58 * 16)!;
    const x0 = Math.round(e.x);
    let fired = false;
    for (let t = 0; t < 400 && !fired; t++) {
      e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
      if (shots.length > 0) fired = true;
    }
    expect(fired).toBe(true);
    expect(Math.abs(e.x - x0)).toBeLessThan(2); // 全程扎根不位移
    expect(shots[0].damage).toBe(10);
    expect(Math.hypot(shots[0].vx, shots[0].vy)).toBeCloseTo(12, 1);
  });

  it('飞行武器 83（aiStyle 23）：三态循环——冲刺(9)→漂移→蓄转(转速递增)', () => {
    const { hooks } = flatWorld({ x: 44, y: 58 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(83, 40 * 16, 58 * 16)!;
    e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(e.ai0).toBe(1);                                  // 冲刺后转漂移
    expect(Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy)).toBeCloseTo(9, 1);
    for (let t = 0; t < 100; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(e.ai0).toBe(2);                                  // 漂移 100t → 蓄转
    const ang0 = e.visAngle;
    e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(e.visAngle).not.toBe(ang0);                      // 转速递增旋转中
  });

133
```

```
  private herplingAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
    if (this.ai2 === 0) { this.ai0 = -100; this.ai2 = 1; }
    else if (this.ai2 > 1) this.ai2 -= 1;
    const derpling = this.vanillaId === 177;
    if (player && !player.dead) this.facing = player.cx > this.cx ? 1 : -1;
    const dist = player ? Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy) : Infinity;
    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
    if (this.onGround && this.vy === 0) {
      this.vx *= 0.8;
      if (Math.abs(this.vx) < 0.1) this.vx = 0;
      if (this.ai3 === this.x && this.ai3 !== 0) { // 原地白跳：转向 + 冷却惩罚
        this.facing *= -1;
        this.ai2 = 300;
      }
      this.ai0 += derpling ? 2 : 5;
      if (player && Number.isFinite(dist) && dist > 0) {
        this.ai0 += Math.trunc((400 / dist) * (derpling ? 5 : 10));
      }
      if (this.ai0 > 30) this.ai0 = 30;
      if (this.ai0 >= 0) {
        if (player && dist > 200 && dist < 350) this.vx += this.facing;
        const bigAt = derpling ? 2 : 3;
        if (this.ai1 === bigAt) {
          this.vy = derpling ? -11.5 : -9;
          this.vx += (derpling ? 2 : 3) * this.facing;
          this.ai0 = -200;
          this.ai1 = 0;
          this.ai3 = this.x;
        } else {
          this.vy = derpling ? -7.5 : -5;
          this.vx += (derpling ? 4 : 5) * this.facing;
          this.ai0 = -120;
          this.ai1 += 1;
        }
      }
    } else {
      const cap = derpling ? 4 : 3;
      if (this.facing === -1 && this.vx > -cap) {
        this.vx -= 0.2;
        if (this.vx > 0) this.vx -= 0.2;
      } else if (this.facing === 1 && this.vx < cap) {
        this.vx += 0.2;
        if (this.vx < 0) this.vx += 0.2;
      }
    }
  }

  /** AI_044 飞鱼 224/巨蚁狮蜂 509/蚁狮蜂 581（NPC.cs:30999-31268）：无重力分轴限速追击。
```

````
Original code: `if (target == 255) { TargetClosest(); ... velocity = dir*num125 }`. This runs every tick until target != 255? target is set by TargetClosest immediately, so it runs once effectively. In our port: use bInit-like flag via WeakMap: first tick set velocity.

- num125: 6; 25→5; 112/666→7.
- 112/666: damage = defDamage (reset); ai0 += 1 clamp 3; if ai0==2 → position += velocity (double move) + dust/sound (SoundID 4,9 → playSfx? game.playSfx('?',...). Which sound? SoundID.Item? I'll use spawnParticles only and skip sound, or use playSfx('killed')? Better skip sound with comment. Actually maybe find a suitable: let me check Sfx names quickly later.
- (112||666) && solid → self-destruct (hurt 9999).
- EncourageDespawn(100) every tick! Important: these NPCs die ~100 ticks after leaving screen. Our despawn system: encourageDespawn(100) sets despawnTimer=min(cur,100)+flag; onscreen resets. OK — call e.encourageDespawn(100) each tick? That would keep despawnTimer at ≤100 always; onscreen reset to 750 then immediately clamped to 100 again. That matches vanilla (timeLeft=100 constant, reset while onscreen... actually vanilla sets timeLeft=100 and CheckActive resets to activeTime when onscreen; net effect same).
- 516: rotation += 0.1*direction; homing steer: v = (v*14 + dir*(|v|+1/12))/15; if |v|<6 → *=1.05; return.
- others: rotation += 0.4*direction; light for fireball (type 25): e.lightRGB = [1, 0.3, 0.1] (only for default branch = type 25 presumably; 30/33 have own dust). I'll set light for 25 only.
- movement: if noTileCollide → x+=vx,y+=vy else moveAndCollide. For 516: collide flags for self-destruct. For 112: SolidCollision check (they have noTileCollide true per json, so SolidCollision never true... but vanilla 1.4.4 VileSpit has noTileCollide? Actually EoW vile spit bounces? no, it passes? whatever — I'll implement: if !noTileCollide → moveAndCollide and use hitWall/onGround as collide; for 112 check solidRect overlap → self-destruct only when !noTileCollide).
````

```
192	  if (biome && VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS[biome as keyof typeof VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS]) {

194	  }
195	  return isDay ? VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.daySurface : VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.nightSurface;
196	}

199	export function biomeAt(st: { type: Uint16Array; idx(x: number, y: number): number; w: number; h: number }, tx: number, ty: number): string | null {
200	  // 从 ty 向下找第一个实心格
201	  for (let y = Math.max(2, ty); y < Math.min(st.h - 2, ty + 60); y++) {
202	    const t = st.type[st.idx(tx, y)];
203	    if (t === 0) continue;
204	    const key = TILE_KEY_NAME[t];
205	    if (!key) return null;
206	    if (key.includes('corrupt') || key === 'ebonstone_block' || key.includes('ebonsand')) return 'corruption';
207	    if (key.includes('crimson') || key.includes('crimsand') || key === 'crimstone') return 'crimson';
208	    if (key === 'mud') return 'jungle';
209	    if (key === 'ice' || key === 'snow') return 'snow';
210	    if (key === 'sand' || key === 'sandstone' || key === 'hardened_sand') return 'desert';
211	    return null;
212	  }
213	  return null;
214	}
```

```
  timeOfDay: number = 0.3;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始上午 7:12 ≈ 0.3）
  dayCount: number = 1;
  /** 月相 0-7（0=满月 4=新月；每黎明 +1 mod 8，Main.cs:64877）。对话月相池/血月门用 */
  moonPhase: number = 0;
  /** 血月旗标（dusk roll / dawn 清除，Main.cs:64817-64831） */
  bloodMoon: boolean = false;
  /** 日食旗标（Main.eclipse，运行时态不进存档）：黎明 roll（Main.cs:64898，
   *  hardMode && downedMechBossAny && 1/20）/ 日耀碑牌召唤（Player.cs:43308，白天），
   *  夜幕清除（Main.cs:64721）——全天持续，只影响白天刷怪段 */
  eclipse: boolean = false;
  /** 圣诞季（现实日期 12/15-31，checkXMas Main.cs:13054） */
  xMas: boolean = false;
  /** 万圣季（现实日期 10/20-11/10，checkHalloween Main.cs:13093） */
  halloween: boolean = false;
  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > 0.25 && this.timeOfDay < 0.75; }
  get dayFactor(): number {
    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
    const t = this.timeOfDay;
    if (t > 0.28 && t < 0.72) return 1;
    if (t >= 0.72 && t < 0.80) return 1 - (t - 0.72) / 0.08 * 0.68;
    if (t >= 0.80 || t < 0.20) return 0.32;
    return 0.32 + (t - 0.20) / 0.08 * 0.68;
  }
  get hourFloat(): number { return this.timeOfDay * 24; }
}

export class World {
  store: TileStore;
  name: string;
  seed: number;
  spawnX = 0; spawnY = 0;
  groundLevel = 0; rockLevel = 0;   // tile 坐标
  /** 地狱顶（原版 UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200；TerrainPass 设定，SceneMetrics/BGM/背景共用） */
  lavaLine = 0;
  clock: Clock;
  /** 天气系统（WeatherState 状态机；存档/wld 导入经 applyWeatherSave 回填） */
  weather = new WeatherState();
  applyWeather(s: Partial<WeatherSave>) { applyWeatherSave(this.weather, s); }
  chests: ChestData[] = [];
  /** 墓碑碑文（原版 Sign 系统的最小子集）：锚点 = 墓碑 tile 左上格 */
  signs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; text: string }> = [];
  /** 读档/wld 导入携带的城镇 NPC（afterWorldLoad 原位生成后清空；原版 WorldFile town NPC 段） */
  pendingTownNpcs?: Array<{ key: string; x: number; y: number; home: [number, number] | null; bound?: boolean; givenName?: string }>;
  // Boss 进度旗标
31:  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > 0.25 && this.timeOfDay < 0.75; }
```

```
// 魔法投射物(原版 Projectile 14 Spark / 短命发光弹语义):
// 直线飞行、撞块消失、命中伤害、寿命短(原版 spark 30tick)。
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import { hitCritters, playEnemyHitSound } from './projTargets';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';

export class MagicProj extends Entity {
  w = 6; h = 6;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  damage: number;
  life: number;
  dead = false;
  color: string;
  grav = 0;

  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, life: number, color: string) {
    super();
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.damage = damage;
    this.life = life;
    this.color = color;
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标)
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    ctx.fillStyle = this.color;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
    if (this.netPuppet) { this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy; return; }
    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
    // 小动物:一击致死并消耗弹体(近战挥砍同语义)
    if (hitCritters(this, game)) { this.dead = true; return; }
    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; dead: boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
      if (e.dead) continue;
      if (this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y) {
        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
        e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);
        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#C080FF');
        this.dead = true;
        return;
      }
    }
```

````
```ts
function solidRect(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): boolean {
  const st = game.world.store;
  const x0 = Math.floor(x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((x + w - 0.01) / TILE);
  const y0 = Math.floor(y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((y + h - 0.01) / TILE);
  for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++)
    if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return true;
  return false;
}
```
````

```
const baseStats = new WeakMap<Enemy, { dmg: number; def: number }>();
const baseOf = (e: Enemy) => {
  let b = baseStats.get(e);
  if (!b) { b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 }; baseStats.set(e, b); }
  return b;
};

/** Enemy 构造注入(Enemy.ts import 时回填,避免循环依赖) */
export function bindEnemyCtor(c: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy']) { enemyCtor = c; }

export function spawnPart(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, id: number): Enemy | null {
  if (!enemyCtor) return null;
  const p = enemyCtor.fromVanilla(id, src.cx, src.cy);
  if (!p) return null;
  return addEnemy(game, p);
}

/** AI_012 骷髅王手(NPC.cs:22289-22400):锚定头(ai1=头 id),头离场 → 自毁;
 *  头非悬浮态 → 漂在头两侧(±120×ai0, 头上方 100);悬浮态 → 缓慢环绕(±200, +230)。
 *  每 300t(ai3 计)朝玩家挥击冲刺。 */
```
